010899:     function withTimeout(promise, timeoutMs, label) {
010900:         return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
010901:             const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
010902:                 reject(
010903:                     new Error(
010904:                         `${label || 'Operation'} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`
010905:                     )
010906:                 );
010907:             }, timeoutMs);
010908: 
010909:             Promise.resolve(promise).then(
010910:                 value => {
010911:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010912:                     resolve(value);
010913:                 },
010914:                 error => {
010915:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010916:                     reject(error);
010917:                 }
010918:             );
010919:         });
010920:     }

020398:     // guard so Unit Finder, Mission Update, visible fallback and Auto Mode all

020408:             .replace(/^Required\s+/i, '')
020409:             .trim();
020410: 
020411:         return /^(?:minimum|min\.?)\b/i.test(withoutRequired);
020412:     }

033455:     async function resumeAutoAfterMainMissionOpen(reason) {
033456:         const started = Date.now();
033457: 
033458:         while (Date.now() - started < 45000) {
033459:             if (isManualAutoStopActive()) return false;
033460: 
033461:             if (isMissionPage() && isMissionScreenVisibleForQueueRestart()) {
033462:                 await waitForMissionReady();
033463: 
033464:                 autoModeRunning = true;
033465:                 sessionStorage.setItem('mf_auto_mode_running', 'true');
033466:                 localStorage.setItem('mf_auto_mode_running', 'true');
033467: 
033468:                 try {
033469:                     updateStatusBox('Mission opened. Auto Mode continuing...');
033470:                 } catch (error) {}
033471: 
033472:                 if (!autoModeLoopActive) {
033473:                     setTimeout(() => {
033474:                         if (
033475:                             autoModeRunning &&
033476:                             !autoModeLoopActive &&
033477:                             !isManualAutoStopActive()
033478:                         ) {
033479:                             runAutoModeLoop();
033480:                         }
033481:                     }, 100);
033482:                 }
033483: 
033484:                 return true;
033485:             }
033486: 
033487:             // A visible empty modal means MissionChief is still loading the mission.
033488:             // Keep waiting, but never open another mission over it.
033489:             if (hasVisibleQueueOpenModal(`resume-${reason}`)) {
033490:                 await wait(500);
033491:                 continue;
033492:             }
033493: 
033494:             // A direct mission URL may appear before the mission content.
033495:             if (/\/missions\/\d+/i.test(window.location.pathname)) {
033496:                 await wait(500);
033497:                 continue;
033498:             }
033499: 
033500:             await wait(500);
033501:         }
033502: 
033503:         if (mfDebugEnabled) {
033504:             debugLog(
033505:                 'MISSION OPEN OWNER',
033506:                 `${reason || 'unknown'} | mission content did not become ready within 45 seconds`
033507:             );
033508:         }
033509: 
033510:         return false;
033511:     }

010902:                 reject(
010903:                     new Error(
010904:                         `${label || 'Operation'} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`
010905:                     )
010906:                 );
010907:             }, timeoutMs);
010908:

010910:                 value => {
010911:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010912:                     resolve(value);
010913:                 },
010914:                 error => {
010915:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010916:                     reject(error);
010917:                 }
010918:             );
010919:         });
010920:     }

012808:     function forceVehicleCheckboxSelection(
012809:         checkbox
012810:     ) {
012811:         if (
012812:             !checkbox ||
012813:             checkbox.disabled ||
012814:             checkbox.isConnected === false
012815:         ) {
012816:             return false;
012817:         }
012818: 
012819:         try {
012820:             const ownerWindow =
012821:                 checkbox.ownerDocument
012822:                     ?.defaultView ||
012823:                 window;
012824: 
012825:             const inputPrototype =
012826:                 ownerWindow.HTMLInputElement
012827:                     ?.prototype;
012828: 
012829:             const checkedDescriptor =
012830:                 inputPrototype
012831:                     ? Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(
012832:                         inputPrototype,
012833:                         'checked'
012834:                     )
012835:                     : null;
--- source 12827-12899 ---
012827:                     ?.prototype;
012828: 
012829:             const checkedDescriptor =
012830:                 inputPrototype
012831:                     ? Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(
012832:                         inputPrototype,
012833:                         'checked'
012834:                     )
012835:                     : null;
012836: 
012837:             if (checkedDescriptor?.set) {
012838:                 checkedDescriptor.set.call(
012839:                     checkbox,
012840:                     true
012841:                 );
012842:             } else {
012843:                 checkbox.checked = true;
012844:             }
012845:         } catch (_error) {
012846:             try {
012847:                 checkbox.checked = true;
012848:             } catch (_innerError) {}
012849:         }
012850: 
012851:         if (checkbox.checked !== true) {
012852:             return false;
012853:         }
012854: 
012855:         dispatchVehicleCheckboxStateEvent(
012856:             checkbox,
012857:             'input'
012858:         );
012859:         dispatchVehicleCheckboxStateEvent(
012860:             checkbox,
012861:             'change'
012862:         );
012863: 
012864:         return checkbox.checked === true;
012865:     }

025959:     function findLegacyVehicleRequirementList() {
025960:         const selectionDocument =
025961:             getVehicleSelectionDocument();
025962:         let vehicleHeadings = [];
025963: 
025964:         try {
025965:             vehicleHeadings =
025966:                 selectionDocument.querySelectorAll(
025967:                     'h4'
025968:                 );
025969:         } catch (_error) {}
025970: 
025971:         for (const heading of vehicleHeadings) {
025972:             if (
025973:                 heading.textContent.trim() !==
025974:                 'Vehicles'
025975:             ) {

006020:     function getPersonnelVehicleQueue(doc, allowedTypeIds) {
006021:         const allowed = new Set((allowedTypeIds || []).map(String));
006022:         const allowAllVehicleTypes = allowed.size === 0;
006023:         const seen = new Set();
006024:         const queue = [];
006025: 
006026:         for (const row of [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')]) {
006027:             const vehicleTypeId = getPersonnelVehicleTypeIdFromRow(row);
006028:             if (!allowAllVehicleTypes && !allowed.has(vehicleTypeId)) continue;
006029: 
006030:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
006031:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006032:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006033:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(link.getAttribute('href') || ''));
006034:             const vehicleId = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
006035:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
006036:                 .find(Boolean) || '';
006037: 
006038:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
006039:             seen.add(vehicleId);
006040: 
006041:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
006042:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''));
006043:             queue.push({
006044:                 vehicleId,
006045:                 vehicleTypeId,
006046:                 name: cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `${TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[vehicleTypeId] || 'Vehicle'} ${vehicleId}`,
006047:                 assignmentHref: directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`,
006048:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
006049:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
006050:             });
006051:         }
006052: 
006053:         queue.sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name, 'en-GB', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' }));
006054:         return queue;
006055:     }

007410:     function parseTrainingCodes(row) {
007411:         const raw = row?.getAttribute('data-filterable-by') ||
007412:             row?.querySelector?.('[data-filterable-by]')?.getAttribute('data-filterable-by') ||
007413:             '[]';
007414:         try {
007415:             const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
007416:             return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.map(String) : [];
007417:         } catch (_error) {
007418:             return String(raw)
007419:                 .replace(/[\[\]"']/g, '')
007420:                 .split(',')
007421:                 .map(value => value.trim())
007422:                 .filter(Boolean);
007423:         }
007424:     }

008766:     function getVehicleQueueFromTable(doc) {

008768:             .map((row, index) => {

008771:                 const vehicleType = TYPE_ID_TO_VEHICLE_TYPE[vehicleTypeId] || '';
008772: 
008773:                 const allLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href^="/vehicles/"]')];
008774: 
008775:                 const editLink = allLinks.find(a => {
008776:                     const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
008777:                     return href.endsWith('/edit');
008778:                 });
008779: 
008780:                 const vehicleLink = allLinks.find(a => {
008781:                     const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
008782:                     return !href.endsWith('/edit') && !href.includes('/zuweisung');
008783:                 });

008785:                 const vehicleHref = vehicleLink?.getAttribute('href') || '';
008786:                 const editHref = editLink?.getAttribute('href') || '';
008787:                 const vehicleId = getVehicleIdFromHref(editHref || vehicleHref);
008788:                 const currentName = cleanText(vehicleLink?.textContent || '');
008789: 
008790:                 return {
008791:                     tableIndex: index,
008792:                     vehicleId,
008793:                     vehicleTypeId,
008794:                     vehicleType,
008795:                     currentName,
008796:                     vehicleHref,
008797:                     editHref
008798:                 };

008801:             .filter(vehicleMatchesSelectedUnitClass);

009053:     // Police attendance, while named trained-personnel requirements remain

009068:     function getMissionEventCollectibleDocuments() {
009069:         const documents = [];
009070:         const seen = new Set();
009071:         const queue = [document];
009072: 
009073:         while (
009074:             queue.length > 0 &&
009075:             documents.length < 24
009076:         ) {
009077:             const candidate = queue.shift();
009078: 
009079:             if (
009080:                 !candidate ||
009081:                 seen.has(candidate)
009082:             ) {
009083:                 continue;
009084:             }
009085: 
009086:             seen.add(candidate);
009087:             documents.push(candidate);
009088: 
009089:             try {
009090:                 candidate
009091:                     .querySelectorAll('iframe')
009092:                     .forEach(frame => {
009093:                         try {
009094:                             const frameDocument =
009095:                                 frame.contentDocument;
009096: 
009097:                             if (
009098:                                 frameDocument &&
009099:                                 !seen.has(frameDocument)
009100:                             ) {
009101:                                 queue.push(frameDocument);
009102:                             }
009103:                         } catch (_error) {}
009104:                     });
009105:             } catch (_error) {}
009106:         }
009107: 
009108:         return documents;
009109:     }

012965:     function sortVehicleCheckboxesByBestArrival(matches) {
012966:         const getMetrics = input => {
012967:             const now = Date.now();
012968:             const cached = mfVehicleArrivalMetricCache.get(input);
012969: 
012970:             if (cached && now < cached.expiresAt) {
012971:                 return cached;
012972:             }
012973: 
012974:             const row = input?.closest?.('tr');
012975:             const metrics = {
012976:                 delay: parseFloat(
012977:                     row?.getAttribute('data-sortvalue') ||
012978:                     row?.getAttribute('timevalue') ||
012979:                     '999999'
012980:                 ),
012981:                 distance: parseFloat(
012982:                     row?.getAttribute('data-distance') ||
012983:                     '999999'
012984:                 ),
012985:                 expiresAt: now + 500
012986:             };
012987: 
012988:             mfVehicleArrivalMetricCache.set(input, metrics);
012989:             return metrics;
012990:         };
012991: 
012992:         matches.sort((a, b) => {
012993:             const metricsA = getMetrics(a);
012994:             const metricsB = getMetrics(b);
012995:             return (
012996:                 metricsA.delay - metricsB.delay ||
012997:                 metricsA.distance - metricsB.distance
012998:             );
012999:         });

017369:     function escapeHtml(value) {
017370:         return String(value)
017371:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
017372:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
017373:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
017374:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
017375:             .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
017376:     }

010899:     function withTimeout(promise, timeoutMs, label) {
010900:         return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
010901:             const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
010902:                 reject(
010903:                     new Error(
010904:                         `${label || 'Operation'} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`
010905:                     )
010906:                 );
010907:             }, timeoutMs);
010908:

010910:                 value => {
010911:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010912:                     resolve(value);
010913:                 },
010914:                 error => {
010915:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
010916:                     reject(error);
010917:                 }
010918:             );
010919:         });
010920:     }

038078:                     'Auto Mode checking the manual Mission Update source...'
038079:                 );

038082:                 // button does when clicked.

038084:                     readMissionUpdateRows();

038087:                     postUnitFinderUpdateRows.length;

038093:                     'AUTO MISSION UPDATE'
038094:                 );

038097:                     handleMissionUpdateUnits(
038098:                         false,
038099:                         postUnitFinderUpdateRows
038100:                     );

038106:                             minimumWait: 100,

038110:                     );
038111:                 }
038112:             }
